# Orca → OpenCLI (Rust): Daftar Pekerjaan Lengkap

Referensi sumber: [stablyai/orca](https://github.com/stablyai/orca) pada commit `b8279cd0` (lihat link per file di bawah).
Target: [Fattan-malva/Open-CLI](https://github.com/Fattan-malva/Open-CLI) branch `master`.

Legenda: ✅ sudah diport ke Rust di OpenCLI · ⬜ belum · 🧪 port butuh tes

## Status ringkas

| Fase | Cakupan | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Hapus orchestrator lama | ✅ selesai (`cff2c39`) |
| 2 | Port logika `shared/orchestration` ke Rust | 🟡 sebagian (lihat bagian A) |
| 2 | Port runtime `main/runtime/orchestration` | ⬜ |
| 2 | Port RPC `main/runtime/rpc/methods/orchestration` | ⬜ |
| 2 | Port CLI `cli/orchestration` | ⬜ |
| 3 | UI: crew, settings orchestration, chat terminal, review diff | ⬜ |
| 4 | Bots, WSL, activity feed | ⬜ |
| 5 | Ukur RAM di Windows (sebelum/sesudah) | ⬜ (dilakukan di mesin Anda) |

- [ ] `cargo test --lib` (Rust) hijau

- [ ] `pnpm exec eslint src` tanpa error baru
